SOTA LP: For current p (f) with M=max f*f, solve LP max ∑g_j s.t. g≥0, (f*g)_l ≤ M for all l. This is linear in g because f fixed. It finds a direction g that can increase sum while cross-correlations with f bounded by M. Rescale to sum 1. This is like finding a point in polar cone? If current f has M, any g satisfying f*g≤M after rescale? Let's analyze. Suppose current p sum=1, M=max p*p. Consider g feasible: g≥0, f*g ≤ M ∑? The LP maximizes sum g under f*g≤M (absolute, not scaled). If rescale g to sum1, cross f*g_rescaled ≤ M / S_g. If S_g >1, then f*g_rescaled < M. Then average t p+(1-t)g? Need bound self-conv: (p+αg)^2? Not trivial. But the SOTA says update f ← (1-t)f+tg, rescale, nonincreasing max. Need verify. If f*g≤M and f*f≤M, then for convex combo h = (1-t)f+tg (with sums maybe adjusted): h*h = (1-t)^2 f*f + t^2 g*g + 2t(1-t) f*g. The cross bounded by M, but g*g can be large. They did not constraint g*g. But maybe extreme point g has bounded g*g? Not guaranteed. The described update rescale g to match normalization then convex combo. If g sum normalized to same, f*g≤M/S_g * S? Let's derive: Let f sum S=1, M_f=max f*f. LP max ∑g s.t. g≥0, f*g≤M_f. Let S_g=∑g. Normalize g'=g/S_g. Then f*g' ≤ M_f/S_g. If S_g≥1, cross ≤M_f. For h=(1-λ)f+λg', h*h=(1-λ)^2 f*f +2λ(1-λ)f*g' +λ^2 g*g'. The last λ^2 g*g' not bounded by M_f unless g*g'≤M_f. Not guaranteed. Maybe LP objective max sum g pushes S_g high and g close to scaled f? Potential algorithms:
- Use `scipy.optimize` L-BFGS-B? Not allowed? scipy allowed general, but only listed modules? It says Use scipy, numpy, cvxpy[...] so yes scipy.optimize likely. But with 1 CPU, time. We can implement custom Adam/gradient to avoid multiple iterations? Need final code robust.

- Since objective scale-invariant, parameterize p in simplex via softmax θ length n. Use smooth max (log-sum-exp) to approximate max convolution. Compute gradient via FFT. Use Adam on θ. Periodically renormalize (softmax does). Use adaptive temperature. Validate exact score.

- Alternatively optimize an unconstrained positive vector w; p=w/sum(w). Objective score=2n max(w*w)/(sum w)^2. Gradient can be computed in w. Then normalize. This avoids softmax Jacobian; easier? Let p=w/S. Objective F=logsumexp(β y) maybe. y = p*p. Gradient wrt p known. Gradient wrt w: p_i=w_i/S; dp_i/dw_j=(δ_{ij}S-p_i)/S^2. Apply similarly: grad_w_j = (1/S^2) [S^2?]. For vector v gradient wrt p, grad_w = (diag(I - (p/S?)?)? Let's derive: p = w/S. ∂p/∂w = (e_j S - w)/S^2 = (e_j - p_j)/S? Actually p_j=w_j/S. derivative p_i wrt w_j = (δ_{ij}S - w_j)/S^2 = (δ_{ij}-p_j)/S. For scalar L(p), grad_w_j = (1/S) ∑_i grad_p_i (δ_{ij}-p_i) = (1/S)[grad_p_j - ∑_i p_i grad_p_i]. So gradient wrt w = (grad_p - mean_p grad_p)/S. Since objective in p homogeneous degree0? y(p) homogeneous degree2, if p scaled by S, y scaled S^2; but logsumexp β y not scale invariant. If optimize w without log p, need include normalization? Score =2n max(w*w)/S^2. Smooth L= logsumexp(β (w*w)/(S^2)) maybe. Or keep p=softmax. Simpler: p=softmax θ; no scaling issues, gradient via p.

But log-sum-exp of y where y small ~1/n; choose β. We can optimize normalized p exactly. Also can add entropy regularization? Maybe.

Alternative: Since objective is max of bilinear, we can use "multiplicative updates" / Frank-Wolfe. For a given active lag set weights w_l, minimize average y_l = p^2 convolved with w? The directional derivative. We can perform convex optimization in p for a quadratic surrogate? Let's consider implementing a custom "smooth log-sum-exp Adam" more controllable and likely effective.

Custom Adam:
```
def smooth_grad(p, beta):
   y=conv(p,p)
   maxy=y.max()
   z=beta*(y-maxy)
   # underflow safe
   ez=np.exp(z-zmax) # zmax=0
   w=ez/ez.sum()
   # h via fft
   wrev=w[::-1]
   P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   W=np.fft.rfft(wrev,N)
   h=np.fft.irfft(P*W,N)[:?]; extract
   gradp=2*beta*h
   # grad_theta
   gtheta=p*(gradp - np.dot(p,gradp))
   # objective value: beta*maxy + log(ez.sum())
   return beta*maxy + np.log(ez.sum()), gtheta, y
```
Note P should be computed once per y. For h, use same P? P length N. But y ifft(P*P) should use `scaling='backward'` default: np.fft.ifft(fft(p)*fft(p)) gives circular convolution of length N; we linear if N>=2n-1. Good. For h, p*wrev linear if N>=3n-2. We use same N>=3n-2, so P same. Good. However `P*W` uses P from p zero-padded to N. Good. Extract h as `hv = np.fft.irfft(P*W, N)` and then h = hv[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]? Need ensure full h length n. Let's test with n small manually. p len n; wrev len 2n-1. conv c length 3n-2. For j=0..n-1, c[2n-2 - j]. That's indices n-1..2n-2. In increasing c, indices n-1 to2n-2 correspond j=n-1 down to0. So h = c[n-1 : 2*n-1][::-1] because end exclusive 2n-1. For n=1, L=1, indices 0..0; c[0:1][::-1] ok. Need ensure if N bigger, c zero-padded; good.

But wrev = w[::-1] length L. Since w length 2n-1, reversed. For j=0, c[2n-2]=sum p_s wrev[2n-2-s]. wrev index 2n-2-s = (2n-2)-(2n-2? length-1? w length L=2n-1, rev index k corresponds w[L-1-k]=w[2n-2-k]. For k=2n-2-s => w[s]. Good.

Objective: The smooth max `L= beta*maxy + log(sum exp(beta(y-maxy)))`. This approximates max_y plus (1/beta)log(K). It is shifted by constant? It tends to max_y + (1/β)log(effective active count). Let's compute derivative of L wrt y: L=βy_max + log(∑ exp(β(y_i-y_max))). Let Z=∑E_i. dL=β e_{max} dy + (1/Z)∑ dE_i. dE_i=E_i(β(δ_{i,max} - δ_{? I made a mistake: L = logsumexp(β y) directly. To compute safely, represent logsumexp(βy) = βmax + log∑ exp(β(y-max)). If we treat max as a constant shift for each function evaluation? The derivative of the *function* log∑exp(β(y-max)) wrt y, with max depending on y, is not simply βw? Let's derive: Let v_i = β(y_i - y_max). Lshift=log∑e^{v_i}. dLshift=∑ e^i(dv_i - max? Now Adam update θ:
```
m = b1*m + (1-b1)*g
v = b2*v + (1-b2)*g^2
step = lr * mhat/(sqrt(vhat)+eps)
theta = theta - step
theta -= theta.mean() # keep mean zero
p = softmax(theta)
```

Implementation details for L-BFGS-B:
```
from scipy.optimize import minimize
def objective(theta):
    p = softmax(theta)
    y = conv_fft(p)
    max_y = y.max()
    z = beta*(y - max_y)
    # if z[0] zero, but sum may overflow if beta*max? We shifted, z≤0, underflow okay
    ez = np.exp(z)
    s = ez.sum()
    val = beta*max_y + np.log(s)
    w = ez/s
    h = conv_weight(p,w)
    gradp = 2*beta*h
    grad = p*(gradp - np.dot(p,gradp))
    return val, grad
```

Let's design exact evaluation wrapper:
```
def safe_evaluate(seq):
    try:
       score=evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except Exception:
       score=np.inf
    return float(score) if np.isfinite(score) else np.inf
```

Convolution internal:
```
def next_pow2(n): return 1 << (int(n)-1).bit_length()
def conv_fft_p(p, y_N=None):
   N = y_N or next_pow2(2*p.size-1)
   P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
   y = np.fft.irfft(P*P, N)[:2*p.size-1]
   return y, P
```
But for gradient with h, need P with N>=3n-2. We'll choose N=next_pow2(3*n-2). Use same for y. For n=2048, N=8192, y okay. For n=4498, N=16384. Use `N = 1 << (3*n - 1).bit_length()`? For n=2048, 6143 ->8192; for n=4498, 13493 ->16384. Good.
`P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)`; `y = np.fft.irfft(P*P, N)[:2n-1]`.
For h: `wrev = w[::-1]`; `W = np.fft.rfft(wrev, N)`; `c = np.fft.irfft(P*W, N)`.
Extract: `h = c[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]`. Need ensure c length N; slice end 2*n-1 <= N? N>=3n-2, so 2n-1 <3n-2 for n>1, okay. For n=1, N? 3n-2=1, N=1? next pow2 maybe1. Slice c[0:1]. works.
Potential floating negative h; not issue. gradp = 2βh. But h may be tiny negative due FFT. We can clip? Not necessary; could cause tiny sign. Maybe `h = np.maximum(h, 0)`? The derivative should be nonnegative if w,p nonnegative. FFT errors can make negative ~1e-17; clipping may improve stability. But if significant negative due invalid N? N correct. Do `h[h < 0 & > -1e-12]=0`; simply `h = np.clip(h, 0, None)`. This enforces nonnegative gradient; objective derivative should nonnegative because p,w nonnegative. Good.

Softmax:
```
def softmax(theta):
    t = theta - np.max(theta)
    e = np.exp(t)
    s = e.sum()
    if s ==0 or not finite: raise
    return e/s
```
If θ large std > 700, exp underflow/overflow handled by subtract max; some zeros okay. But if only one large, p spike. L-BFGS-B may push; okay.

Objective function should be thread-safe; no.

But L-BFGS-B will call with θ arrays possibly not centered; we can center inside fun? If fun modifies θ? We can make a copy and subtract mean for stability: Since softmax invariant to constant shift, we can evaluate at `t0 = theta - np.mean(theta)` to prevent drift. But gradient wrt original θ? Adding a constant shift has zero gradient; if we pass centered values, gradient same. Good. This can improve robustness. In fun: `xmax = x - x.mean()` (copy). But x may be large; subtracting mean cancels. However if x has huge values, mean huge; good. Then p=softmax(xmax).

To avoid objective becoming huge due β*max_y ~ factor <= few hundred, finite. val ~ βM + logS; for β=64n, M~1/n, βM~64. Good.

```
Implementation:
```
def make_objective(p? n? beta):
    N=...
    def fun(x):
       x0 = x - np.mean(x)
       p = softmax(x0)
       P = rfft(p,N)
       y = irfft(P*P,N)[:L]
       # small negative clip y
       if not finite? return inf with zero grad
       maxy = y.max()
       z = beta*(y - maxy)
       # z max 0
       ez = exp(z) # z negative, underflow okay; if all zero? max 1, so not.
       s = ez.sum()
       val = beta*maxy + log(s)
       w = ez/s
       act = flatnonzero(w > 1e-6)
       if act.size <= 128:
          h=zeros(n)
          for l in act: ... add w[l]*p[...]
       else:
          wrev=w[::-1]; W=rfft(wrev,N); c=irfft(P*W,N); h=c[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]; h=np.clip(h,0,None)
       gradp = 2*beta*h
       g = p*(gradp - np.dot(p,gradp))
       # numerical cleanup
       g[~finite]=0
       if not finite(val): return (inf, np.zeros(x.shape))
       return (float(val), g)
    return fun
```
Potential: `np.exp(z)` when z has large negative underflows to0; okay. `s` at least1 because max z=0 (one lag), so log finite. If multiple max, s≥2.

The val approximates maxy + (logS)/β, not exactly max, but okay. For optimization, at high β val close to max. We could also set val = maxy if beta high? No, gradient uses logsumexp. Use val consistent.
```

def _start_list(n, seed):
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
   starts=[]
   # base sinusoid shifts
   for sh in [0.0,0.05,-0.05,0.1,-0.1,0.2]:
      f=1 - np.sin(2*np.pi*(t+sh)) + 1e-3
      f=np.maximum(f,1e-8)
      starts.append(np.log(f/f.sum()))
   # decays
   for k in [0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0,5.0]: starts.append(-k*t)
   for k in [1,2,4,8]: starts.append(-k*t*t)
   # random smooth
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   for amp in [0.5,1.0,2.0]:
      th=np.zeros(n)
      for m in range(1,6):
         th += amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*t + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
      th -= 0.5*t
      starts.append(th)
   # maybe remove too uniform if ever close to constant via finite diff? not needed.
   return starts

Need `project_simplex` efficient:
```
def project_simplex(v):
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    n=len(v); cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1)-1) > (cssv-1))[0][-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/ (rho+1)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Standard. Ensure sum close1; adjust normalize.

Custom Adam implementation:
```
def adam_improve(n, theta, beta_factors, steps, lr, N, deadline):
   m=np.zeros_like(theta); v=np.zeros_like(theta); t=0
   for bf in beta_factors:
      beta=bf*n
      fun=make_objective(fn,beta,N)
      for i in range(steps):
         if time>deadline-10: break
         val,g=fun(theta)
         if not finite: break
         t +=1; m=0.9*m+0.1*g; v=0.999*v+0.999*g*g
         th=m/(1-0.9**t); vh=v/(1-0.999**t)
         theta = theta - lr*th/(np.sqrt(vh)+1e-9)
         theta = theta - theta.mean()
         # every 20 eval exact
   return theta
```

```
import numpy as np, time, math
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _sp_minimize
except Exception:
    _sp_minimize=None

def _npow2(m): ...

def _softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)): ...
    x -= np.max(x)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return np.full_like(x,1/ len(x))
    return e/s

def _conv_sq(p,N):
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
    y=np.fft.irfft(P*P,N)
    y=y[:2*p.size-1]
    y[y<0]=0.0 # maybe small, but if negative large? clip
    return y

def _score_fft(p,N):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0: return inf
    if abs(s-1)>1e-12: p=p/s
    y=_conv_sq(p,N)
    return 2*p.size*y.max()

def _project_simplex(v): ...
def _active_dir(p,K=24):
    n=p.size; y=_conv_sq(p,N?) Need N for score? In polish, use N global.
```

Now, active polish `project_simplex` after p - step*d may produce q sum1. The projection algorithm: For standard simplex, we project v onto conv hull? The formula with v not necessarily sum1. It returns nonnegative sum1. Good. If v contains NaN, return p.

Projection code:
```
def _project_simplex(v):
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1)-1) > cssv-1)[0]
    if rho.size==0: return np.zeros(n)
    rho=rho[-1]
    theta=(cssv[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
    q=np.maximum(v-theta,0)
    s=q.sum()
    if s>0 and finite: q/=s
    return q
```
This projects onto {x>=0,sum=1}. It assumes v finite.

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _sp_minimize
except Exception:
    _sp_minimize = None

def _next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```
Need if m is np int? int(m).

Softmax:
```
def _softmax(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0: return x
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.nan_to_num(x, nan=0.0, posinf=50.0, neginf=-50.0)
    x = x - np.max(x)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(x.shape, 1.0/x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e/s
```

Conv square:
```
def _conv_square(p,N):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2*p.size - 1
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    y = np.fft.irfft(P*P, N)
    y = y[:L]
    # small negatives
    maxy = y.max() if y.size else 0.0
    if maxy >= 0:
        y[y < 0.0] = 0.0
    return y
```
If negative large? FFT error only. okay.

Score fft:
```
def _score_fft(p,N):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size==0: return np.inf
    s=p.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <=0: return np.inf
    if abs(s-1) > 1e-12:
       p = p/s
    y=_conv_square(p,N)
    m=y.max()
    if not np.isfinite(m): return np.inf
    return 2.0*p.size*m
```

Starts:
```
def _make_starts(n, rng):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n,endpoint=False)
    starts=[]
    def add(theta):
       theta=np.asarray(theta,dtype=np.float64)
       if theta.size != n: return
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(theta)): theta=np.nan_to_num(...)
       # avoid constant? no
       starts.append(theta - np.mean(theta))
    eps=1e-4
    for sh in [0.0, 0.04, -0.04, 0.08, -0.08, 0.12]:
        f=1 - np.sin(2*np.pi*(t+sh)) + eps
        f=np.maximum(f,eps)
        add(np.log(f))
    # macro pattern and reverse
    macro=np.array([0.3,0.3,0.1,0.1,0.2])
    for arr in [macro, macro[::-1]]:
        x=np.linspace(0,1,a.size,endpoint=True)
        f=np.interp(t, x, arr) + 1e-5
        add(np.log(f))
    for k in [0.5,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0,5.0]:
        add(-k*t)
    for k in [1,2,4,8]:
        add(-k*t*t)
    # random low-freq
    for _ in range(4):
       th = -0.5*t
       for m in range(1,6):
          th += rng.normal(0,1.0) * np.sin(2*np.pi*m*t + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       add(th)
    # maybe shuffle remove duplicates? not necessary
    return starts[:12]
```

Objective:
```
def _make_objective(n,beta,N):
    L=2*n-1
    def fun(x):
       x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
           return (1e100, np.zeros(x.shape, dtype=np.float64))
       x = x - np.mean(x)
       p=_softmax(x)
       P=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
       y=np.fft.irfft(P*P,N)[:L]
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
           return (1e100, np.zeros(x.shape, dtype=np.float64))
       y[y<0]=0
       maxy=float(y.max())
       if not np.isfinite(maxy):
           return (1e100, np.zeros(x.shape, dtype=np.float64))
       z=beta*(y - maxy)
       z=np.minimum(z,0.0)
       ez=np.exp(z)
       s=ez.sum()
       if s<=0 or not np.isfinite(s):
           val=1e100; grad=np.zeros_like(x); return val,grad
       w=ez/s
       # active
       act = np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-6)
       if act.size <= max(64, N // 32):
           h=np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
           for li in act:
               l=int(li); wl=float(w[l])
               lo=max(0, l-n+1); hi=min(n-1,l)
               if hi>=lo:
                   h[lo:hi+1] += wl * p[l-hi : l-lo+1][::-1]
       else:
           wrev=w[::-1]
           W=np.fft.rfft(wrev,N)
           c=np.fft.irfft(P*W,N)
           h=c[n-1:2*n-1][::-1]
           h=np.clip(h,0.0,None)
       gradp=2.0*beta*h
       mean_pg=float(np.dot(p, gradp))
       g=p*(gradp - mean_pg)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
           g=np.nan_to_num(g, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
       val=beta*maxy + float(np.log(s))
       if not np.isfinite(val): val=1e100
       return (float(val), g)
    return fun
```
Potential direct h loop with large act maybe 256; okay. If p has tiny values, p slice okay.

```
def _active_direction(p,N,K=24):
   n=p.size; L=2*n-1
   y=_conv_square(p,N)
   if y.size<K: idx=np.arange(L)
   else: idx=np.argpartition(y, -K)[-K:]
   h=np.zeros(n)
   for li in idx: ... uniform weight 1.0
   gradp=2.0*h/len(idx)
   d=gradp - gradp.mean()
   sd=d.std()
   if sd>0: d=d/sd
   else: d=np.zeros(n)
   return d
```

```
def _polish(p, t_deadline, seed):
   p=np.asarray(p,float).copy(); p=np.clip(p,0,None); s=p.sum(); if s<=0: return p
   p=p/s; n=p.size; N=_next_pow2(3*n-2)
   best=p.copy(); best_sc=_score_fft(p,N)
   rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
   steps=(1e-5,3e-5,1e-4,3e-4,1e-3,3e-3,1e-2,3e-2)
   max_iter = 1000 if n<=512 else 400
   stagnant=0
   for it in range(max_iter):
       if time.monotonic() > t_deadline: break
       # active
       d=_active_direction(p,N,24)
       if np.any(d !=0):
           improved=False
           for st in steps[::2]: # every other
               q=p - st*d
               q=_project_simplex(q)
               sc=_score_fft(q,N)
               if sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                   best=q; best_sc=sc; p=q.copy(); improved=True; stagnant=0; break
           if improved: continue
       stagnant += 1
       # random if stagnant or every few
       if it % 3 == 0 or stagnant > 10:
           width=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//200), max(3,n//20)))
           start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,n-width+1)))
           q=p.copy()
           sigma = 0.2 + 0.3*((it//10)%3)*0.2? simpler rng.uniform(0.05,0.5)
           q[start:start+width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, rng.uniform(0.05,0.5))))
           q=np.clip(q,0,None); s=q.sum()
           if s>0:
              q/=s
              sc=_score_fft(q,N)
              if sc < best_sc:
                   best=q.copy(); best_sc=sc; stagnant=0
                   if it % 20 ==0: p=q.copy()
   return best
```

```
def _run_min(fun, x0, options):
    try: return _sp_minimize(fun, x0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options=options)
    except Exception:
       def f_sep(x):
          val,g=fun(x); return val
       def g_sep(x):
          val,g=fun(x); return g
       return _sp_minimize(f_sep, x0, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=g_sep, options=options)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize as _sp_minimize
except Exception:
    _sp_minimize = None


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _finite_vec(x, limit=50.0):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(x)):
        x = np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
        x[x > limit] = limit
        x[x < -limit] = -limit
    return x


def _softmax(x):
    x = _finite_vec(x, 50.0)
    if x.size == 0:
        return x
    x = x - np.max(x)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(x.shape, 1.0 / x.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _conv_square(p, N):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    L = 2 * p.size - 1
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    y = np.fft.irfft(P * P, N)
    y = y[:L]
    if y.size and y.max() >= 0:
        y[y < 0.0] = 0.0
    return y


def _score_fft(p, N):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    s = p.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return np.inf
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        p = p / s
    y = _conv_square(p, N)
    m = y.max() if y.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.inf
    return 2.0 * p.size * float(m)


def _project_simplex(v):
    v = _finite_vec(v)
    n = v.size
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([1.0])
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cssv = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.nonzero(u * (np.arange(1, n + 1) - 1) > (cssv - 1))[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    rho = int(idx[-1])
    theta = (cssv[rho] - 1.0) / (rho + 1.0)
    q = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    s = q.sum()
    if s > 0 and np.isfinite(s):
        q /= s
    else:
        q[:] = 1.0 / n
    return q


def _make_starts(n, rng):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, endpoint=False)
    starts = []

    def add(theta):
        theta = _finite_vec(np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64))
        if theta.size != n:
            return
        starts.append(theta - float(np.mean(theta)))

    eps = 1e-4

    for sh in (0.0, 0.04, -0.04, 0.08, -0.08, 0.12):
        f = 1.0 - np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * (t + sh)) + eps
        f = np.maximum(f, eps)
        add(np.log(f))

    macro = np.array([0.30, 0.30, 0.10, 0.10, 0.20])
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, macro.size, endpoint=True)
    for arr in (macro, macro[::-1]):
        f = np.interp(t, x, arr) + 1e-5
        add(np.log(f))

    for k in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0, 3.0, 5.0):
        add(-k * t)

    for k in (1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        add(-k * t * t)

    for _ in range(4):
        th = -0.5 * t
        for m in range(1, 6):
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            th += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * t + ph)
        add(th)

    return starts[:12]


def _make_objective(n, beta, N):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    direct_limit = max(64, N // 32)

    def fun(x):
        x = _finite_vec(x)
        x = x - float(np.mean(x))
        p = _softmax(x)

        P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
        y = np.fft.irfft(P * P, N)[:L]
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
            return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)
        y[y < 0.0] = 0.0
        maxy = float(y.max())
        if not np.isfinite(maxy):
            return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)

        z = beta * (y - maxy)
        z = np.minimum(z, 0.0)
        ez = np.exp(z)
        s = ez.sum()
        if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
            return 1e100, np.zeros_like(x)

        w = ez / s
        act = np.flatnonzero(w > 1e-6)

        if act.size <= direct_limit:
            h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for li in act:
                l = int(li)
                wl = float(w[l])
                lo = max(0, l - n + 1)
                hi = min(n - 1, l)
                if hi >= lo:
                    h[lo:hi + 1] += wl * p[l - hi : l - lo + 1][::-1]
        else:
            wrev = w[::-1]
            W = np.fft.rfft(wrev, N)
            c = np.fft.irfft(P * W, N)
            h = c[n - 1:2 * n - 1][::-1]
            h = np.clip(h, 0.0, None)

        gradp = 2.0 * beta * h
        mean_pg = float(np.dot(p, gradp))
        g = p * (gradp - mean_pg)
        g = _finite_vec(g)

        val = beta * maxy + float(np.log(s))
        if not np.isfinite(val):
            val = 1e100
        return float(val), g

    return fun


def _run_minimize(fun, x0, options):
    try:
        return _sp_minimize(fun, x0, method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True, options=options)
    except Exception:
        def f_sep(x):
            val, _ = fun(x)
            return val

        def g_sep(x):
            _, g = fun(x)
            return g

        return _sp_minimize(f_sep, x0, method="L-BFGS-B", jac=g_sep, options=options)


def _active_direction(p, N, K=24):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    y = _conv_square(p, N)
    L = y.size
    if K >= L:
        idx = np.arange(L)
    else:
        idx = np.argpartition(y, -K)[-K:]

    yy = y[idx]
    s = float(yy.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    ww = yy / s

    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for j, li in enumerate(idx):
        l = int(li)
        wl = float(ww[j])
        lo = max(0, l - n + 1)
        hi = min(n - 1, l)
        if hi >= lo:
            h[lo:hi + 1] += wl * p[l - hi : l - lo + 1][::-1]

    gradp = 2.0 * h
    d = gradp - float(np.mean(gradp))
    sd = float(d.std())
    if sd > 0 and np.isfinite(sd):
        d /= sd
    else:
        d = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    return _finite_vec(d)


def _polish_sequence(p, t_deadline, seed):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
    s = p.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return p
    p = p / s

    n = p.size
    N = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
    best = p.copy()
    best_sc = _score_fft(p, N)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    max_iter = 1000 if n <= 512 else 450
    steps = (1e-5, 3e-5, 1e-4, 3e-4, 1e-3, 3e-3, 1e-2, 3e-2)
    stagnant = 0

    for it in range(max_iter):
        if time.monotonic() > t_deadline:
            break

        d = _active_direction(p, N, 24)
        if np.any(d != 0.0):
            improved = False
            for st in steps:
                q = p - st * d
                q = _project_simplex(q)
                sc = _score_fft(q, N)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best = q.copy()
                    best_sc = sc
                    p = q.copy()
                    improved = True
                    stagnant = 0
                    break
            if improved:
                continue
            stagnant += 1

        if it % 3 == 0 or stagnant > 10:
            width = int(rng.integers(max(2, n // 200), max(3, n // 20)))
            if width >= n:
                width = max(1, n // 4)
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - width + 1)))
            q = p.copy()
            sigma = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.55))
            q[start:start + width] *= float(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma)))
            q = np.clip(q, 0.0, None)
            qs = q.sum()
            if qs > 0 and np.isfinite(qs):
                q /= qs
                sc = _score_fft(q, N)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                    best = q.copy()
                    best_sc = sc
                    p = q.copy()
                    stagnant = 0

    return best


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget_s = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget_s = 1300.0
    t0_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget_s - 5.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    best_p = None
    best_score = np.inf
    best_theta = None
    best_theta_n = None

    def consider(p, theta=None, theta_n=None):
        nonlocal best_p, best_score, best_theta, best_theta_n
        if p is None:
            return
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
        if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
            return
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = p.sum()
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
            return
        p = p / s
        try:
            sc = evaluate_sequence(p.tolist())
        except Exception:
            return
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = float(sc)
            best_p = p.copy()
            if theta is not None:
                best_theta = _finite_vec(np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64))
                best_theta_n = int(p.size)

    try:
        parent = height_sequence_1
        parent = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
        if parent.ndim == 1 and parent.size > 0 and np.isfinite(parent).all():
            consider(parent)
    except Exception:
        pass

    consider(np.array([0.30, 0.30, 0.10, 0.10, 0.20], dtype=np.float64))

    def optimize_theta_sequential(theta, n, N, schedule, min_deadline_margin=30.0):
        cur = _finite_vec(np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64))
        cur = cur - float(np.mean(cur))
        cur_p = _softmax(cur)
        cur_sc = _score_fft(cur_p, N)
        consider(cur_p, cur, n)

        for mult, iters in schedule:
            if time.monotonic() > t0_deadline - min_deadline_margin:
                break
            fun = _make_objective(n, float(mult) * float(n), N)
            try:
                res = _run_minimize(
                    fun,
                    cur,
                    options={
                        "maxiter": int(iters),
                        "maxls": 6,
                        "ftol": 1e-10,
                        "gtol": 1e-6,
                        "maxfun": max(120, int(iters) * 6),
                        "m": 10,
                    },
                )
                new = getattr(res, "x", None)
                if new is None:
                    continue
                new = _finite_vec(np.asarray(new, dtype=np.float64))
                new = new - float(np.mean(new))
                p_new = _softmax(new)
                sc_new = _score_fft(p_new, N)
                consider(p_new, new, n)
                if (
                    np.all(np.isfinite(new))
                    and np.isfinite(sc_new)
                    and sc_new <= cur_sc * 1.10 + 1e-12
                ):
                    cur = new
                    cur_sc = sc_new
            except Exception:
                pass
        return cur

    if _sp_minimize is None:
        def adam_run(theta, n, N, schedule, steps=120, lr=0.005):
            cur = _finite_vec(np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64))
            cur = cur - float(np.mean(cur))
            m = np.zeros_like(cur)
            v = np.zeros_like(cur)
            tcount = 0
            for mult, _it in schedule:
                fun = _make_objective(n, float(mult) * float(n), N)
                for _ in range(steps):
                    if time.monotonic() > t0_deadline - 25.0:
                        break
                    val, g = fun(cur)
                    if not np.isfinite(val):
                        break
                    tcount += 1
                    m = 0.9 * m + 0.1 * g
                    v = 0.999 * v + 0.999 * (g * g)
                    m_hat = m / (1.0 - 0.9 ** tcount)
                    v_hat = v / (1.0 - 0.999 ** tcount)
                    cur = cur - lr * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + 1e-8)
                    cur = _finite_vec(cur)
                    cur -= float(np.mean(cur))
                    if tcount % 10 == 0:
                        consider(_softmax(cur), cur, n)
            return cur

    else:
        if time.monotonic() < t0_deadline - 180.0:
            small_n = 64
            Ns = _next_pow2(3 * small_n - 2)
            small_starts = _make_starts(small_n, rng)[:3]
            small_sched = [(6, 25), (24, 35), (96, 40)]
            for st in small_starts:
                if time.monotonic() > t0_deadline - 120.0:
                    break
                optimize_theta_sequential(st, small_n, Ns, small_sched, min_deadline_margin=50.0)

    if time.monotonic() < t0_deadline - 120.0:
        if budget_s >= 900:
            n = 2048
        elif budget_s >= 350:
            n = 1024
        else:
            n = 512
        N = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
        starts = _make_starts(n, rng)

        max_starts = 5 if n <= 1024 else 4
        big_sched = [(6, 30), (24, 50), (96, 60), (300, 55)]

        for st in starts[:max_starts]:
            if time.monotonic() > t0_deadline - 90.0:
                break
            opt_theta = optimize_theta_sequential(
                st, n, N, big_sched, min_deadline_margin=45.0
            )
            consider(_softmax(opt_theta), opt_theta, n)

    if best_theta is not None and time.monotonic() < t0_deadline - 90.0:
        n_fin = int(best_theta_n) if best_theta_n is not None else int(best_p.size)
        If_n = next_pow2 if (if_n := lambda m: _next_pow2(m)) else None
        Nfin = _next_pow2(3 * n_fin - 2)
        optimize_theta_sequential(
            best_theta,
            n_fin,
            Nfin,
            [(300, 50), (800, 40)],
            min_deadline_margin=35.0,
        )

    if best_p is not None and time.monotonic() < t0_deadline - 20.0:
        polished = _polish_sequence(best_p, t0_deadline - 12.0, seed + 1)
        consider(polished)

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = np.array([0.30, 0.30, 0.10, 0.10, 0.20], dtype=np.float64)

    final_p = np.clip(np.asarray(best_p, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
    final_score = evaluate_sequence(final_p.tolist())
    if not np.isfinite(final_score) or final_p.size == 0:
        final_p = np.array([0.30, 0.30, 0.10, 0.10, 0.20], dtype=np.float64)

    return [float(x) for x in final_p]
```
